namespace = libra_fairy

# Alicis hates people who change this file

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0001
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_country_type = default
	}

	immediate = {
		libra_country_check = yes
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				not = { has_leader_flag = has_libra_inital_effect }
			}
			set_leader_flag = has_libra_inital_effect
			libra_leader_inital_effect = yes
		}
		switch = {
			trigger = has_origin
			origin_shroudwalker_apprentice = {
				add_resource = { sr_zro = 50 }
			}
			origin_libra_praises_to_life = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0100 }
			}
			origin_libra_psalm_of_waves = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0200 }
			}
			origin_libra_ultimate_market = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0300 }
			}
			origin_libra_enlightenment_wisdom = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0400 }
			}
			origin_libra_crimson_nights = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0500 }
			}
			origin_libra_shadow_consciousness = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0600 }
			}
			origin_libra_dragonborn = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0700 }
			}
			origin_libra_paradox_loop = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0800 }
			}
			origin_libra_paradise_lose = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.0900 }
			}
			origin_libra_interstellar_port = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1000 }
			}
			origin_libra_antientropy_instruction = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1100 }
			}
			origin_libra_planetarian = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1200 }
			}
			origin_libra_eden_accord = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1300 }
			}
			origin_libra_polemics_mechanism = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1400 }
			}
			origin_libra_miracle_builder = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1500 }
			}
			origin_libra_may_storm = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1600 }
			}
			origin_libra_extreme_iteration = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1700 }
			}
			origin_libra_centurion = {
				country_event = { id = libra_origin.1800 }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				country_event = { id = libra_main.0020 days = 25200 }
			}
			3 = {
				modifier = {
					set = 0
					or = {
						is_ironman = yes
						is_multiplayer = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = libra_main.0020 days = 25920 random = 1440 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = libra_fairy.0002

		has_global_flag = game_started
		from = { is_hidden = no }

		from = {
			set_leader_flag = has_libra_inital_effect
			libra_leader_inital_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0003
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				species = { is_exact_same_species = from }
			}
			update_libra_leader_traits_after_modification = yes
		}
		every_pool_leader = {
			limit = {
				species = { is_exact_same_species = from }
			}
			update_libra_leader_traits_after_modification = yes
		}
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0004
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = owner
		any_owned_pop_group = {
			is_sapient = yes
			species = {
				check_modifier_value = {
					modifier = libra_colony_start_species_pops
					value > 0
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				is_sapient = yes
				species = {
					check_modifier_value = {
						modifier = libra_colony_start_species_pops
						value > 0
					}
				}
			}
			root = {
				create_pop_group = {
					species = prev
					size = prev.species.modifier:libra_colony_start_species_pops
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trait = trait_libra_lithoid_5
				}
				root = {
					add_deposit = d_buried_lithoids
					add_deposit = d_buried_lithoids
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_trait = trait_libra_infernal_2
				}
				root = {
					add_planet_devastation = 35
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { not = { has_planet_flag = libra_colonized } }
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = { is_sapient = yes }
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait = trait_libra_psychic_4
					}
					root = {
						add_deposit = d_libra_feature_6
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0007
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = owner
		not = { has_planet_flag = libra_colonized }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_planet_flag = libra_colonized
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_tradition = tr_libra_adventure_1 }
				every_owned_leader = {
					limit = {
						leader_class = official
						has_base_skill < 10
					}
					add_experience = 120
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = root.sector
						root.sector.sector_capital = { exists = leader }
					}
					root.sector.sector_capital.leader = {
						add_experience = 120
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_tradition = tr_libra_ceremony_1
					has_country_flag = libra_ceremony_1_effect
				}
				remove_country_flag = libra_ceremony_1_effect
				change_variable = { which = libra_ceremony_count value = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0008
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = fromfrom
		is_controlled_by = from
		not = { uses_district_set = cosmogenesis_world }
		fromfrom = {
			not = {
				has_purge_type = { type = purge_displacement }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			change_variable = {
				which = libra_pop_purged_count
				value = local_pop_amount
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_tradition = tr_libra_havoc_finish }
				add_resource = {
					energy = 2
					trade = 2
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_astral_blade }
				add_resource = {
					astral_threads = 3
					mult = local_pop_amount
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_string_puppeteer }
				add_resource = {
					energy = 3
					trade = 1
					mult = local_pop_amount
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_technology = tech_libra_nanite_industry_association }
				add_resource = {
					engineering_research = 5
					nanites = 5
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0010
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = from
		not = { has_planet_flag = libra_recently_terraformed }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_timed_planet_flag = {
			flag = libra_recently_terraformed
			days = 1800
		}
		from = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_tradition = tr_libra_frugality_4 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = physics_research value = 6 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = society_research value = 6 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = engineering_research value = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_tradition = tr_libra_construmcracy_1 }
				add_council_agenda_progress = 750
			}
		}
	}
}

ship_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0011
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = owner
	}

	immediate = {
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_tradition = tr_libra_ceremony_1
					has_country_flag = libra_ceremony_1_effect
				}
				remove_country_flag = libra_ceremony_1_effect
				change_variable = { which = libra_ceremony_count value = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_tradition = tr_libra_construmcracy_3
				}
				add_resource = { influence = 15 }
			}
		}
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0015
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		From = {
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			not = { has_country_flag = libra_disable_rat }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		while = {
			limit = { has_building = building_fe_clinic_1 }
			remove_building = building_fe_clinic_1
		}
		while = {
			limit = { has_building = building_fe_clinic_2 }
			remove_building = building_fe_clinic_2
		}
		while = {
			limit = { has_building = building_fe_assembly_1 }
			remove_building = building_fe_assembly_1
		}
		while = {
			limit = { has_building = building_fe_assembly_2 }
			remove_building = building_fe_assembly_2
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0499
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_variable_set = libra_ea_path
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_transcendent_covenant_a_finish_modifier
		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_transcendent_covenant_b_finish_modifier
		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_transcendent_covenant_c_finish_modifier

		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_1
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_auth_democratic
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_auth_oligarchic
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_auth_dictatorial
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_auth_imperial
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_auth_corporate
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_auth_default

		remove_modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_0
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_1
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_3
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_4
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_5
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_6
		every_playable_country = {
			limit = {
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = root modifier = opinion_libra_third_choice }
			}
			remove_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_libra_third_choice who = root }
		}

		remove_modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_1
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_3

		remove_modifier = ap_libra_sages_notes_modifier

		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_kings_game_effect
		clear_variable = libra_kings_game_count

		every_owned_planet = {
			remove_building = building_libra_galaxy_bureaucratic_1
			remove_building = building_libra_galaxy_bureaucratic_2
			remove_building = building_libra_galaxy_bureaucratic_3
			remove_building = building_libra_galaxy_bureaucratic_4
		}

		every_owned_leader = {
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_ultimate_directive_ruler
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_ultimate_directive_growth
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_ultimate_directive_cognitive
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_ultimate_directive_legion
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_ultimate_directive_regulatory
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_commander
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_official
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_scientist
		}

		every_situation = {
			limit = {
				or = {
					is_situation_type = situation_libra_gestalt_era_scientist
					is_situation_type = situation_libra_gestalt_era_official
					is_situation_type = situation_libra_gestalt_era_commander
					is_situation_type = situation_libra_sages_notes
				}
			}
			destroy_situation = this
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0500
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_transcendent_covenant
		is_variable_set = libra_ea_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_ea_path value = 1 }
	}

	immediate = {
		add_council_agenda_progress_percent = 0.1
		country_event = { id = libra_fairy.0500 days = 240 random = 120 }
		random = {
			chance = 80
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = {
					has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10
				}
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
				root = {
					create_message = {
						type = LIBRA_TRANSCENDENT_COVENANT_EFFECT
						localization = LIBRA_TRANSCENDENT_COVENANT_EFFECT_2_DESC
						days = 18
						target = prev
						variable = {
							type = name
							localization = PLANET
							scope = prev
						}
					}
				}
			}
			break = yes
		}
		create_message = {
			type = LIBRA_TRANSCENDENT_COVENANT_EFFECT
			localization = LIBRA_TRANSCENDENT_COVENANT_EFFECT_1_DESC
			days = 18
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0501
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_transcendent_covenant
		is_variable_set = libra_ea_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_ea_path value = 1 }
	}

	immediate = {
		every_owned_planet = {
			limit = {
				has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
			}
		}
		capital_scope = {
			create_pop_group = { species = root }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0509
	title = libra_fairy.0509.name
	desc = libra_fairy.0509.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_galactic_community
	show_sound = event_administrative_work

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.0509.a
		trigger = {
			capital_scope = { has_planetary_ascension_tier < 8 }
		}
		capital_scope = {
			custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.0509.a.tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = 8
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.0509.b
		add_council_agenda_progress = 10000
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.0509.c
		add_resource = { influence = 75 }
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = unity
			value = 6
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0519
	title = libra_fairy.0519.name
	desc = libra_fairy.0519.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_civil_action
	show_sound = event_administrative_work

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.0519.a
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = unity
			value = 12
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.0519.b
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = physics_research
			value = 12
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0520
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_shift_in_focus
		is_variable_set = libra_ea_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_ea_path value = 3 }
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_1
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_2
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_modifier_value = { modifier = country_government_civic_points_add value < 4 }
			}
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_1
				multiplier = modifier:country_government_civic_points_add
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_1
				multiplier = 4
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_unused_civic_points < 2
			}
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_2
				multiplier = trigger:has_unused_civic_points
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = ap_libra_shift_in_focus_modifier_2
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

				add_modifier = { modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_3 multiplier = libra_third_choice_mult }
			}
			libra_third_choice_d = {
				add_modifier = { modifier = ap_libra_third_choice_modifier_4 multiplier = libra_third_choice_mult }

	}

			}

					is_war_participant = { war = fromfrom side = root }
				}

			}
		}
		from = {

				flag = libra_third_choice_effect
				days = 2
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0550
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_gestalt_era
	}

	immediate = {
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_gestalt_era_scientist
			target = capital_scope
			effect = {
				set_situation_approach = situation_libra_gestalt_era_scientist_a
			}
		}
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_gestalt_era_official
			target = capital_scope
			effect = {
				set_situation_approach = situation_libra_gestalt_era_official_a
			}
		}
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_gestalt_era_commander
			target = capital_scope
			effect = {
				set_situation_approach = situation_libra_gestalt_era_commander_a
			}
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			switch = {
				trigger = leader_class
				scientist = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_scientist
						show_message = no
					}
					root = {
						change_variable = {
							which = libra_gestalt_era_scientist_skill_count
							value = prev.trigger:has_total_skill
						}
					}
				}
				official = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_official
						show_message = no
					}
					root = {
						change_variable = {
							which = libra_gestalt_era_official_skill_count
							value = prev.trigger:has_total_skill
						}
					}
				}
				commander = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_commander
						show_message = no
					}
					root = {
						change_variable = {
							which = libra_gestalt_era_commander_skill_count
							value = prev.trigger:has_total_skill
						}
					}	
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_1
			multiplier = libra_gestalt_era_scientist_skill_count
		}
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_2
			multiplier = libra_gestalt_era_official_skill_count
		}
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_3
			multiplier = libra_gestalt_era_commander_skill_count
		}
		clear_variable = libra_gestalt_era_scientist_skill_count
		clear_variable = libra_gestalt_era_official_skill_count
		clear_variable = libra_gestalt_era_commander_skill_count
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.0551
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_variable_set = libra_ea_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_ea_path value = 6 }
	}

	immediate = {
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = { leader_class = scientist }
			if = {
				limit = { not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_scientist } }
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_scientist
					show_message = no
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = libra_gestalt_era_skill_mult value < 10 } }
				change_variable = { which = libra_gestalt_era_skill_mult value = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
				add_experience = 200
			}
		}
	}
}

				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_libra_gestalt_era_commander
					show_message = no
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = libra_gestalt_era_skill_mult value < 10 } }
				change_variable = { which = libra_gestalt_era_skill_mult value = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
				add_experience = 200
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = libra_fairy.0556
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_variable_set = libra_ea_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_ea_path value = 6 }
	}

		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				has_base_skill <= 9999
				leader_class = commander
				or = {
					is_hidden = no
					is_councilor = yes
				}
			}
			root = {
				change_variable = {
					which = libra_gestalt_era_commander_skill_count
					value = prev.trigger:has_total_skill
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
				add_experience = 100
			}
		}
		remove_modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_3
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = ap_libra_gestalt_era_modifier_3
			multiplier = libra_gestalt_era_commander_skill_count
		}
		clear_variable = libra_gestalt_era_commander_skill_count
	}
}

			if = {
				limit = {
					is_part_of_galactic_council = yes
					position_on_last_resolution = support
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_authoritarian
					}
				}
				remove_modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_authoritarian_effect
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_authoritarian_effect
					days = 1800
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_materialist
					}
				}
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = physics_research value = 3 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = society_research value = 3 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = engineering_research value = 3 }
				remove_modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_materialist_effect
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_materialist_effect
					days = 1800
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					position_on_last_resolution = abstain
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_xenophobe
					}
				}
				add_resource = { influence = 135 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = unity value = 6 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_commerce
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_industry
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_market
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_greatergood
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_ecology
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_galactic_focus
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_politics_tradition_1
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_politics_tradition_2
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_politics_tradition_3
						last_resolution_category_changed = resolution_category_politics_tradition_4
					}
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_egalitarian
					}
				}
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = unity value = 12 }
				remove_modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_egalitarian_effect
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_egalitarian_effect
					days = 2880
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.1006
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				position_on_last_resolution = oppose
				any_owned_leader = {
					is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_corrupt_destiny_a
				}
			}
			add_resource = { influence = 300 }
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					leader_class = official
					has_base_skill < 10
				}
				add_experience = 1000
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				position_on_last_resolution = oppose
				any_owned_leader = {
					is_assigned_to_galcom = yes

				}
			}
			random_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
					has_leader_flag = libra_enable_resolution_fail_effect
				}
				switch = {
					trigger = has_trait
					leader_trait_libra_synthetic_official_delegate_d = {
						prev = {
							add_resource = { influence = 75 }
							every_owned_leader = {
								limit = {
									is_councilor = yes
									has_base_skill < 10
								}

							}
						}
					}

						prev = {
							add_resource = { influence = 150 }
							every_owned_leader = {
								limit = {
									is_councilor = yes
									has_base_skill < 10
								}
								add_experience = 480
							}
						}
					}
				}
				switch = {
					trigger = has_trait
					leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_corrupt_destiny_b = {
						prev = {
							add_resource = { influence = 100 }
							add_council_agenda_progress_percent = 0.75
							if = {
								limit = { diplomacy_weight > 200000 }
								add_resource = { trade = 100000 }
							}
							else = {
								add_resource = {
									trade = 0.5
									mult = trigger:diplomacy_weight
								}
							}
						}
					}
					leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_destiny_d = {
						root = {
							add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_libra_official_delegate_destiny_d who = prevprev }
						}
						prev = {
							random_owned_leader = {
								limit = { has_base_skill < 7 }
								add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1
							}
							random_owned_leader = {
								limit = { has_base_skill < 9 }
								add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { is_galactic_community_member = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_part_of_galactic_council = yes
					position_on_last_resolution = oppose
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_authoritarian
					}
				}
				remove_modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_authoritarian_effect
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_authoritarian_effect
					days = 1800
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_materialist
					}
				}
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = physics_research value = 3 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = society_research value = 3 }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = engineering_research value = 3 }
				remove_modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_materialist_effect
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_materialist_effect
					days = 1800
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					position_on_last_resolution = abstain
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_assigned_to_galcom = yes
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_official_delegate_xenophobe

					is_owned_by = root

				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		change_variable = {
			which = libra_pop_purged_count
			value = value:libra_commander_general_xenophobe_effect_mult
		}
		add_resource = {
			unity = 8
			influence = 0.03
			mult = value:libra_commander_general_xenophobe_effect_mult
		}
		while = {
			count = value:libra_commander_general_xenophobe_effect_mult
			from = { add_opinion_modifier = { who = prev modifier = opinion_genocidal_our_species } }
		}
		ordered_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				has_base_skill < 10
				leader_class = commander
			}
			position = 0
			order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
			inverse = yes
			add_experience = fromfrom.trigger:pop_amount
		}
		FromFrom = {
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				kill_pop_group = {
					pop_group = this
					percentage = 0.5
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		every_ground_combat_defender = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_defensive_army = yes }
				damage_army = 240
			}
			else = {
				damage_army = 720
			}
		}
	}
}

ship_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.1031
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = leader
		leader = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_scientist_explorer_corrupt_destiny_b }
	}

	immediate = {
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = { from = { is_star = yes } }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = physics_research value = 1.5 max = 120000 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { from = { is_asteroid = yes } }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = engineering_research value = 0.5 max = 40000 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { from = { is_astral_scar = yes } }
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = society_research value = 7.5 max = 600000 }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.1520
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = energy value = 12 min = 2400 }
		add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = minerals value = 12 min = 2400 }
		add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = food value = 12 min = 2400 }
		add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = influence value = 12 min = 120 }
		change_variable = { which = libra_ceremony_count value = 3 }
		while = {
			count = 3
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = {
					has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10
				}
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
				weights = {
					base = 10
					modifier = {
						subtract = trigger:has_planetary_ascension_tier
						has_planetary_ascension_tier > 5
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.1521
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		not = { has_country_flag = libra_fairy_1521_triggered }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = libra_fairy_1521_triggered
			days = 3600
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				has_base_skill < 10
				can_have_libra_special_councilor_traits = yes
			}
			add_experience = root.value:libra_ceremony_exp_reward
		}
		while = {
			count = 3
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = {
					has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10
				}
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
				weights = {
					base = 10
					modifier = {
						subtract = trigger:has_planetary_ascension_tier
						has_planetary_ascension_tier > 5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_variable_set = libra_ceremony_count
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_ceremony_count
					value > 0
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_origin = origin_libra_praises_to_life }
				change_variable = { which = libra_praises_to_life_count value = libra_ceremony_count }
			}
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = alloys value = 1 mult = libra_ceremony_count }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = consumer_goods value = 1 mult = libra_ceremony_count }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = sr_living_metal value = 1 mult = libra_ceremony_count }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = sr_zro value = 1 mult = libra_ceremony_count }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = sr_dark_matter value = 1 mult = libra_ceremony_count }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = nanites value = 1 mult = libra_ceremony_count }
			change_variable = { which = libra_ceremony_effect value = libra_ceremony_count }
			remove_modifier = libra_ceremony_effect_modifier
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = libra_ceremony_effect_modifier
				multiplier = libra_ceremony_effect
			}
			divide_variable = { which = libra_ceremony_count value = 6 }
			floor_variable = libra_ceremony_count
			while = {
				count = libra_ceremony_count
				random_owned_leader = {
					limit = {
						has_base_skill <= 9999
						not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_ceremony }
						can_have_libra_special_councilor_traits = yes
					}
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_ceremony
					}
					weights = {
						base = 1
						modifier = {
							factor = 2
							has_base_skill > 3
						}
						modifier = {
							factor = 2
							has_base_skill > 6
						}
						modifier = {
							factor = 4
							or = {
								is_ruler = yes
								is_leader_tier = leader_tier_renowned
								is_leader_tier = leader_tier_legendary
							}
						}
						modifier = {
							factor = 0.1
							is_hidden = yes
							is_councilor = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = { which = libra_ceremony_count value = 0 }
		}
	}
}

		remove_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_official
		remove_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_commander
		remove_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_scientist
		if = {
			limit = { exists = ruler }
			ruler = {
				switch = {
					trigger = leader_class
					official = { root = { set_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_official } }
					commander = { root = { set_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_commander } }
					scientist = { root = { set_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_scientist } }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				1 = { set_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_official }
				1 = { set_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_commander }
				1 = { set_country_flag = libra_imperium_ruler_scientist }
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.1610
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		not = { has_country_flag = libra_fairy_1610_triggered }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = libra_fairy_1610_triggered
			days = 1800
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				leader_years_of_service > 49
				or = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist
				}
			}
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist
			switch = {
				trigger = leader_class
				commander = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander_max show_message = no }
				}
				official = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official_max show_message = no }
				}
				scientist = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist_max show_message = no }
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				nor = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander_max
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official_max
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist_max
				}
				can_have_libra_special_leader_traits = yes
			}
			position = 0
			order_by = trigger:leader_years_of_service
			if = {
				limit = { leader_years_of_service > 49 }
				switch = {
					trigger = leader_class
					commander = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander_max show_message = yes }
					}
					official = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official_max show_message = yes }
					}
					scientist = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist_max show_message = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				switch = {
					trigger = leader_class
					commander = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander show_message = yes }
					}
					official = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official show_message = yes }
					}
					scientist = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist show_message = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			which = libra_grandmastery_count
			value = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_tradition = tr_libra_grandmastery_5 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_variable_set = libra_grandmastery_count
					check_variable = {
						which = libra_grandmastery_count
						value < 15
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = libra_grandmastery_5_modifier
					multiplier = libra_grandmastery_count
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = libra_grandmastery_count }
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = libra_grandmastery_5_modifier
					multiplier = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist
				}
			}
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official
			remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist
			switch = {
				trigger = leader_class
				commander = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander_max show_message = no }
				}
				official = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official_max show_message = no }
				}
				scientist = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist_max show_message = no }
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				has_base_skill <= 9999
				nor = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander_max
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official_max
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist_max
				}
				can_have_libra_special_leader_traits = yes
			}
			position = 0
			order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
			if = {
				limit = { leader_years_of_service > 49 }
				switch = {
					trigger = leader_class
					commander = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander_max show_message = yes }
					}
					official = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official_max show_message = yes }
					}
					scientist = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist_max show_message = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				switch = {
					trigger = leader_class
					commander = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander show_message = yes }
					}
					official = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official show_message = yes }
					}
					scientist = {
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist show_message = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			root = {
				change_variable = {
					which = libra_grandmastery_count
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_variable_set = libra_grandmastery_count
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_grandmastery_count
					value < 15
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = libra_grandmastery_5_modifier
				multiplier = libra_grandmastery_count
			}

					which = libra_profundity_1
					value > 59
				}
			}
			subtract_variable = {
				which = libra_profundity_1
				value = 60
			}
			while = {
				count = 60
				ordered_owned_leader = {
					limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
					position = 0
					order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
					#inverse = yes
					add_experience = 600
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			while = {
				count = libra_profundity_1
				ordered_owned_leader = {
					limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
					position = 0
					order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
					#inverse = yes
					add_experience = 600
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				which = libra_profundity_1
				value = 0
			}
		}
	}
}

				}
			}
			else = {
				libra_create_shield_machine_effect = yes

					save_event_target_as = libra_leader_to_set
				}
			}
		}
		assign_leader = event_target:libra_leader_to_set
	}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.5001
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = owner
	}

	immediate = {
		owner = {
			add_resource = {
				physics_research = 0.4
				society_research = 0.4
				engineering_research = 0.4
				unity = 0.2
				trade = 0.6
				mult = root.trigger:pop_amount
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { pop_amount < 12500 }
			export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
				trigger = pop_amount
				variable = libra_hive_survival_competition_count
			}
			divide_variable = { which = libra_hive_survival_competition_count value = 500 }
			floor_variable = libra_hive_survival_competition_count
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				which = libra_hive_survival_competition_count
				value = 25
			}
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = { pop_group_size > 1 }
			kill_pop_group = {
				pop_group = this
				percentage = 0.2
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = libra_hive_survival_competition_modifier
			days = 3600
			multiplier = libra_hive_survival_competition_count
			clear_on_owner_change = yes
		}
		clear_variable = libra_hive_survival_competition_count
	}
}

situation_event = {

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	situation = this

	trigger = {
		owner = {
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		switch = {
			trigger = has_situation_flag
			libra_directive_76_unlock_starbase = {
				situation_event = { id = libra_fairy.10031 }
			}
			libra_directive_76_unlock_building = {
				situation_event = { id = libra_fairy.10032 }
			}
			libra_directive_76_unlock_species_trait = {
				situation_event = { id = libra_fairy.10033 }
			}
			libra_directive_76_unlock_policy = {
				situation_event = { id = libra_fairy.10034 }
			}
			libra_directive_76_unlock_decision = {
				situation_event = { id = libra_fairy.10035 }
			}
			libra_directive_76_unlock_leader_trait = {
				situation_event = { id = libra_fairy.10036 }
			}
		}
	}
}

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.10031
	title = libra_fairy.10031.name
	desc = libra_fairy.10031.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_genetic_ascention_cloning
	show_sound = event_engineered_evolution
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	trigger = {
		owner = {
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_situation_flag = libra_directive_76_unlock_starbase
		owner = {
			set_country_flag = libra_directive_76_starbase
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				every_owned_starbase = {
					limit = {
						or = {
							has_starbase_size > starbase_starhold
							has_starbase_size >= orbital_ring_tier_2
							has_starbase_size = starbase_deep_space_citadel_2
							has_starbase_size = starbase_deep_space_citadel_3
						}
					}
					set_starbase_building = { slot = 1 building = libra_directive_76_garrison }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = GOOD
		custom_tooltip = libra_directive_76_starbase_tooltip
	}
}

			}
		}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.11001
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 20 }
		set_variable = { which = libra_babel_level value = 0 }
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_rebuilding_babel
			target = capital_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			every_owned_starbase = {
				limit = { has_starbase_size > starbase_starhold }
				set_starbase_building = { slot = 2 building = libra_babel_tower }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.11002
	title = libra_fairy.11002.name
	desc = libra_fairy.11002.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_ode_to_babel_1
	show_sound = event_engineered_evolution

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
		num_buildings = {
			type = building_libra_babel_tower
			value = 0
			in_construction = any
		}
		nor = {
			has_country_flag = can_build_libra_babel_tower
			has_country_flag = libra_rebuilding_babel
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		start_situation = {

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.12002
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
	}

	immediate = {
		country_event = { id = libra_fairy.12002 days = 360 random = 360 }
		create_message = {
			type = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT
			localization = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT_DESC
			days = @toast_message_days
			variable = {
				type = key
				value = leader_trait_libra_evolutionary_endpoint
				localization = TRAIT
			}
			variable = {
				key = TRAIT_KEY
				value = leader_trait_libra_evolutionary_endpoint
			}
			variable = {
				key = "border"
				value = "GFX_invisible"
			}
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				has_base_skill <= 9999
				species = { is_robotic = no }
				can_have_libra_special_ruler_traits = yes
				nor = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_psionic
					has_trait = leader_trait_chosen
					has_trait = leader_trait_cyborg
					has_trait = leader_trait_limited_cyborg
					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_evolutionary_endpoint
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 35
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_libra_evolutionary_endpoint
					show_message = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.12114
	title = libra_fairy.12114.name
	desc = libra_fairy.12114.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_cutefied_world_1
	show_sound = event_engineered_evolution
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	immediate = {
		set_situation_locked = yes
		owner = {
			create_species = {
				is_mod = yes
				class = "LIBRACUTE"
				portrait = random
				name = this
				plural = this
				gender = this
				namelist = this
				homeworld = this
				can_be_modified = yes
				clear_parent_species_link = yes
				traits = {
					trait = trait_libra_cutefied
				}
				effect = {
					save_event_target_as = libra_cutefied_species
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_hive_empire = yes }
				modify_species = {
					species = last_created_species
					add_trait = trait_hive_mind
					effect = { save_event_target_as = libra_cutefied_species }
				}
			}

			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					is_sapient = yes
					is_enslaved = no
					is_being_purged = no
					is_robot_pop_group = no
				}
				change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
			}
			every_controlled_ship = {
				limit = {
					is_ship_class = shipclass_colonizer
				}
				change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
			}
			every_owned_army = {
				limit = {
					exists = species
					or = {
						army_type = assault_army
						army_type = clone_army
						army_type = gene_warrior_army
						army_type = abomination_army
					}
				}
				modify_army = {
					name = random
					type = gene_warrior_army
					species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
				}
			}
			every_owned_army = {
				limit = {
					exists = species
					exists = planet
					army_type = defense_army
				}
				modify_army = {
					name = random
					type = defense_army
					species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
				}
			}
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					is_hidden = no
					is_event_leader = no
					species = { is_robotic = no }
					is_leader_tier = leader_tier_default
				}
				change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
				change_leader_portrait = species
				random = {
					chance = 65
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
						show_message = no
					}
				}
			}
			every_pool_leader = {
				limit = {
					species = { is_robotic = no }
				}
				change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
				change_leader_portrait = species
				random = {
					chance = 65
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
						show_message = no
					}
				}
			}
			every_envoy = {
				change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
				change_leader_portrait = species
			}
			create_message = {
				type = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT
				localization = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT_DESC
				days = @toast_message_days
				variable = {
					type = key
					value = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
					localization = TRAIT
				}
				variable = {
					key = TRAIT_KEY
					value = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
				}
				variable = {
					key = "border"
					value = "GFX_invisible"
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
				every_owned_leader = {
					limit = { is_councilor = yes }
					set_gestalt_node_portrait_effect = yes
				}
			}
			change_dominant_species = { species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species }
		}
	}

	after = {
		destroy_situation = this
		owner = {
			start_situation = {
				type = situation_libra_cutefied_world_2
				target = capital_scope
			}
			custom_tooltip = situation_libra_cutefied_world_1_tooltip
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.12114.a
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.12114.b
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.12151
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_cutefied_world_2
			target = capital_scope
			effect = { set_situation_approach = situation_libra_cutefied_world_2_a }
		}
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = libra_cutefied_world_a
			days = 5400
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_species_class = LIBRACUTE
				has_trait = trait_libra_cutefied
			}
			species = { save_event_target_as = libra_cutefied_species }
		}
		else = {
			create_species = {
				is_mod = yes
				class = "LIBRACUTE"
				portrait = random
				name = this
				plural = this
				gender = this
				namelist = this
				homeworld = this
				can_be_modified = yes
				clear_parent_species_link = yes
				traits = {
					trait = trait_libra_cutefied
				}
				effect = { save_event_target_as = libra_cutefied_species }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_hive_empire = yes }
				modify_species = {
					species = last_created_species
					add_trait = trait_hive_mind
					effect = { save_event_target_as = libra_cutefied_species }
				}
			}
			change_dominant_species = { species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species }
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				is_sapient = yes
				is_enslaved = no
				is_being_purged = no
				is_robot_pop_group = no
			}
			change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_hidden = no
				is_event_leader = no
				species = {
					is_robotic = no
					not = { is_species_class = LIBRACUTE }
				}
				is_leader_tier = leader_tier_default
			}
			change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
			change_leader_portrait = species
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied }
				}
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
					show_message = no
				}
			}
		}
		every_pool_leader = {
			limit = {
				species = {
					is_robotic = no
					not = { is_species_class = LIBRACUTE }
				}
				not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied }
			}
			change_species = event_target:libra_cutefied_species
			change_leader_portrait = species
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied }
				}
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
					show_message = no
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = { is_councilor = yes }
				set_gestalt_node_portrait_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.12153
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_cutefied_world_2
			target = capital_scope
			effect = { set_situation_approach = situation_libra_cutefied_world_2_c }
		}
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = libra_cutefied_world_c
			days = 3600
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = { has_base_skill <= 9999 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied }
				}
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
					show_message = no
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
					check_variable = { which = libra_cutefied_level value < 10 }
				}
				change_variable = { which = libra_cutefied_level value = 1 }
			}
		}
		every_pool_leader = {
			limit = { has_base_skill <= 9999 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied }
				}
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
					show_message = no
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
					check_variable = { which = libra_cutefied_level value < 10 }
				}
				change_variable = { which = libra_cutefied_level value = 1 }
			}
		}
		create_message = {
			type = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT
			localization = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT_DESC
			days = @toast_message_days
			variable = {
				type = key
				value = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
				localization = TRAIT
			}
			variable = {
				key = TRAIT_KEY
				value = leader_trait_libra_cutefied
			}
			variable = {
				key = "border"
				value = "GFX_invisible"
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
		}
	}

				}

			}
		}
	}

	}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.14001
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_cosmological_constant

			which = libra_cosmological_tech
			value = trigger:num_researched_techs
		}
	}

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.14010
	title = libra_fairy.14010.name
	desc = libra_fairy.14010.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_cosmological_constant_2
	show_sound = event_the_flesh_is_weak
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	immediate = {
		set_situation_locked = yes
		owner = {
			subtract_variable = {
				which = libra_cosmological_tech
				value = trigger:num_researched_techs
			}
			divide_variable = { which = libra_cosmological_tech value = -5 }
			floor_variable = libra_cosmological_tech
			while = {
				count = libra_cosmological_tech
				random_owned_leader = {
					limit = {
						has_base_skill <= 9999
						species = { is_robotic = yes }
						nor = {
							has_trait = leader_trait_psionic
							has_trait = leader_trait_chosen
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_cosmological_constant
						}
					}
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_cosmological_constant show_message = yes }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = libra_cosmological_tech value > 9 }
				}
				root = {
					set_variable = { which = libra_cosmological_constant value = 10 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				root = {
					set_variable = {
						which = libra_cosmological_constant
						value = prev.libra_cosmological_tech
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				which = libra_cosmological_tech
				value = trigger:num_researched_techs
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.14010.a
		owner = {
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = energy value = 6 min = 12000 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = food value = 6 min = 12000 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = minerals value = 6 min = 12000 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = consumer_goods value = 6 min = 9000 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = alloys value = 6 min = 6000 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = trade value = 6 min = 9000 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = unity value = 6 min = 6000 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = influence value = 6 min = 100 }
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { current_situation_approach = situation_libra_cosmological_constant_approach_a }	
				owner = {
					start_situation = {
						type = situation_libra_cosmological_constant
						target = capital_scope
						effect = { set_situation_approach = situation_libra_cosmological_constant_approach_a }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				owner = {
					start_situation = {
						type = situation_libra_cosmological_constant
						target = capital_scope
						effect = { set_situation_approach = situation_libra_cosmological_constant_approach_b }
					}
				}
			}
			destroy_situation = this
		}
	}
}

			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = society_research value = 6 }
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = engineering_research value = 6 }

		}

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.16013
	title = libra_fairy.16013.name
	desc = libra_fairy.16013.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_gray_goo
	show_sound = event_robot_encounter
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	trigger = {
		owner = {
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_situation_locked = yes
		owner = {
			capital_scope = {
				create_fleet = {
					effect = {
						set_owner = prevprev
						set_variable_to_random_value = { which = libra_random min = 32 max = 40 rounded = yes }
						while = {
							count = libra_random
							while = {
								count = 4
								create_ship = {
									random_existing_design = nanite_swarmer
									prefix = yes
								}
							}
							create_ship = {
								random_existing_design = nanite_interdictor
								prefix = yes
							}
						}
						set_location = prev
						clear_variable = libra_random
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.16013.a
		custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.16013.tt
	}

	after = {
		set_situation_locked = no
		owner = {
			add_council_agenda_progress = 2500
		}
	}
}

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.16014
	title = libra_fairy.16014.name
	desc = libra_fairy.16014.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_gray_goo
	show_sound = event_robot_encounter
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	trigger = {
		owner = {
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_situation_locked = yes
		owner = {
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					has_base_skill <= 9999
					species = { is_robotic = yes }
					nand = {
						has_trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_legendary
						is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level
						check_variable = { which = libra_nanocore_level value > 49 }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level }
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { which = libra_nanocore_level value < 50 }
						}
						change_variable = { which = libra_nanocore_level value = 1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_renowned }
						random_list = {
							0 = {
								modifier = {
									add = libra_nanocore_level
								}
								set_leader_tier = leader_tier_legendary
								remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_renowned
								add_trait = {
									trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_legendary
									show_message = yes
								}
							}
							200 = { }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							nor = {
								has_trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_renowned
								has_trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_legendary
							}
						}
						random_list = {
							0 = {
								modifier = {
									add = libra_nanocore_level
								}
								libra_renowned_leader_effect = yes
								add_trait = {
									trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_renowned
									show_message = yes
								}
							}
							150 = { }
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { not = { has_leader_flag = has_libra_extra_veteran_trait } }
					switch = {
						trigger = leader_class
						official = {
							add_trait = {
								trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_official
								show_message = no
							}
						}
						commander = {
							add_trait = {
								trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_commander
								show_message = no
							}
						}
						scientist = {
							add_trait = {
								trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_scientist
								show_message = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.16014.a
		custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.16014.tt
	}

	after = {
		set_situation_locked = no
		owner = {
			add_council_agenda_progress = 2500
		}
	}
}

					factor = 2

				}

				limit = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_renowned }

				}

		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.16110

	desc = "libra_fairy.16110.desc"

	show_sound = event_radio_chatter
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_hurricane_eye_count
			value = value:libra_num_ships|SIZE|nanite_swarmer|MULT|1|
		}
		change_variable = {
			which = libra_hurricane_eye_count
			value = value:libra_num_ships|SIZE|nanite_interdictor|MULT|15|
		}
		every_owned_fleet = {
			limit = {
				is_mobile = yes
				is_leased = no
				is_in_combat = no
				is_ship_class = shipclass_military
				nor = {
					exists = leader
					has_fleet_order = merge_fleet_order
				}
			}
			every_owned_ship = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						is_ship_size = nanite_swarmer
						is_ship_size = nanite_interdictor
					}
				}
				destroy_ship = this
			}
		}
		subtract_variable = {
			which = libra_hurricane_eye_count
			value = value:libra_num_ships|SIZE|nanite_swarmer|MULT|1|
		}
		subtract_variable = {
			which = libra_hurricane_eye_count
			value = value:libra_num_ships|SIZE|nanite_interdictor|MULT|15|
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = GOOD
	}

	after = {
		add_resource = {
			nanites = 300
			mult = libra_hurricane_eye_count
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_variable = libra_hurricane_eye_count
		}
	}
}

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.16120
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	immediate = {
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_planet = { has_modifier = libra_hurricane_eye_modifier }
				}
				random_owned_planet = {
					limit = { has_modifier = libra_hurricane_eye_modifier }
					save_event_target_as = libra_hurricane_eye_planet
				}
			}
			else = {
				capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = libra_hurricane_eye_planet }
			}
			event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_planet = {
				solar_system = { save_event_target_as = libra_hurricane_eye_system }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_policy_flag = libra_hurricane_eye_1_b
					root = { not = { current_situation_approach = situation_libra_hurricane_eye_d } }
				}
				random_owned_starbase = {
					limit = {
						exists = solar_system
						has_starbase_size = juggernaut
						or = {
							solar_system = { is_owned_by = prevprev }
							and = {
								solar_system = { has_owner = no }
								prev = { has_resource = { type = influence amount > 50 } }
							}
							and = {
								solar_system = {
									has_owner = yes
									is_owned_by = prevprev
								}
								prev = { has_resource = { type = influence amount > 100 } }
							}
						}
					}
					solar_system = {
						save_event_target_as = libra_hurricane_eye_system
						if = {
							limit = { has_owner = yes }
							if = {
								limit = { not = { is_owned_by = prevprev } }
								prevprev = { add_resource = { influence = -100 } }
								owner = {
									if = {
										limit = {
											not = { has_tradition = tr_nanotech_finish }
											or = {
												is_country_type = default
												is_country_type = fallen_empire
												is_country_type = awakened_fallen_empire
											}
										}
										add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = opinion_libra_hurricane_eye who = prevprevprev }
									}
								}
							}
						}
						else = {
							prevprev = { add_resource = { influence = -50 } }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		switch = {
			trigger = current_situation_approach
			default = {
				spawn_random_storm = {
					type = libra_hurricane_eye_storm_1
					immediate = yes
					cosmic_storm_start_position = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_system
				}
				last_created_cosmic_storm = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_storm_type = libra_hurricane_eye_storm_1 }
						event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_system = {
							system_event = {
								id = libra_fairy.16122
								days = 360
								scopes = { from = prev }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				owner = {
					start_situation = {
						type = situation_libra_hurricane_eye
						target = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_planet
						effect = {
							set_situation_approach = situation_libra_hurricane_eye_a
						}
					}
				}
			}
			situation_libra_hurricane_eye_b = {
				spawn_random_storm = {
					type = libra_hurricane_eye_storm_2
					immediate = yes
					cosmic_storm_start_position = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_system
				}
				owner = {
					start_situation = {
						type = situation_libra_hurricane_eye
						target = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_planet
						effect = { set_situation_approach = situation_libra_hurricane_eye_b }
					}
				}
			}
			situation_libra_hurricane_eye_c = {
				spawn_random_storm = {
					type = libra_hurricane_eye_storm_3
					immediate = yes
					cosmic_storm_start_position = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_system
				}
				last_created_cosmic_storm = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_storm_type = libra_hurricane_eye_storm_3 }
						lock_storm_in_place = yes
						event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_system = {
							system_event = {
								id = libra_fairy.16122
								days = 359
								scopes = { from = prev }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				owner = {
					start_situation = {
						type = situation_libra_hurricane_eye
						target = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_planet
						effect = { set_situation_approach = situation_libra_hurricane_eye_c }
					}
				}
			}
			situation_libra_hurricane_eye_d = {
				owner = {
					start_situation = {
						type = situation_libra_hurricane_eye
						target = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_planet
						effect = {
							set_situation_approach = situation_libra_hurricane_eye_d
							set_situation_flag = libra_hurricane_eye_d
						}
					}
					capital_scope.solar_system = {
						create_ambient_object = {
							type = "libra_hurricane_eye_storm"
							location = this.star
						}
						last_created_ambient_object = {
							set_ambient_object_flag = libra_hurricane_eye
						}
						system_event = {
							id = libra_fairy.16123
							days = 360
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		destroy_situation = this
	}
}

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.16130
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	immediate = {
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_planet = { has_modifier = libra_hurricane_eye_modifier }
				}
				random_owned_planet = {
					limit = { has_modifier = libra_hurricane_eye_modifier }
					save_event_target_as = libra_hurricane_eye_planet
				}
			}
			else = {
				capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = libra_hurricane_eye_planet }
			}
			start_situation = {
				type = situation_libra_hurricane_eye_2
				target = event_target:libra_hurricane_eye_planet
				effect = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							prev = {
								check_variable = { which = libra_hurricane_eye_fleet_total value > 9999 }
							}
						}
						set_variable = { which = libra_hurricane_fleet value = 25 }
					}
					else = {
						set_variable = {
							which = libra_hurricane_fleet
							value = prev.libra_hurricane_eye_fleet_total
						}
						divide_variable = {
							which = libra_hurricane_fleet
							value = 400
						}
						floor_variable = libra_hurricane_fleet
					}
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = libra_fairy.16131 days = 1 }
		}
		destroy_situation = this
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.16901
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
			trigger = count_owned_pop_amount
			parameters = {
				limit = { is_robot_pop = no }
			}
			variable = libra_nanite_locusts_pop_count
			rounded = yes
		}
		change_variable = { which = libra_nanite_locusts_pop_count value = 0 }
		owner = {
			add_resource = {
				engineering_research = 8
				mult = root.libra_nanite_locusts_pop_count
			}
			change_variable = {
				which = nanotech_worlds_created
				value = 1
			}
			change_variable = {
				which = libra_nanite_locusts_count
				value = 1
			}
			remove_modifier = libra_nanite_locusts_modifier
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = libra_nanite_locusts_modifier
				mult = libra_nanite_locusts_count
			}
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = { is_robot_pop = no }
			kill_pop_group = {
				pop_group = this
				percentage = 1
			}
		}
		cancel_terraformation = yes
		set_planet_flag = colony_event
		remove_modifier = being_devoured_modifier_nanotech
		set_timed_planet_flag = {
			days = 360
			flag = recently_eaten_planet
		}
		divide_variable = {
			which = libra_nanite_locusts_pop_count
			value = 100
		}
		while = {
			count = libra_nanite_locusts_pop_count
			create_army = {
				owner = owner
				species = owner_main_species
				type = libra_nanite_locusts_army
			}
		}
		clear_variable = libra_nanite_locusts_pop_count
		add_deposit = d_nanotech_devastation
		while = {
			count = trigger:planet_size
			add_deposit = d_nanotech_devastation
		}
		create_pop_group = {
			species = owner_main_species
			size = 25
		}
		planet_event = { id = action.68 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.17000
	title = libra_fairy.17000.name
	desc = libra_fairy.17000.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_yggdrasil_2
	show_sound = event_robot_encounter

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.17000.a
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = ap_libra_yggdrasil_modifier
			days = 5400
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.17001
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 80 }
		add_resource = { consumer_goods = 5000 }
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_yggdrasil
			target = capital_scope
			effect = { set_situation_approach = situation_libra_yggdrasil_a }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.17002
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_count value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_yggdrasil_count
					value > 2
				}
			}
			set_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_count value = 0 }
			random_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					has_base_skill <= 9999
					has_trait = leader_trait_virtual
					not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_yggdrasil }
					can_have_libra_special_ruler_traits = yes
				}
				add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_yggdrasil show_message = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.17003
	title = libra_fairy.17003.name
	desc = libra_fairy.17003.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_yggdrasil_2
	show_sound = event_robot_encounter

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
		check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				has_base_skill <= 9999
				has_trait = leader_trait_virtual
				not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_yggdrasil }
				can_have_libra_special_ruler_traits = yes
			}
			add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_yggdrasil show_message = yes }
		}
	}

	after = {
		custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.17003.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = building_libra_yggdrasil_foundry_1
		hidden_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_foundry_mult value = 3 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = building_libra_yggdrasil_factory_1
		hidden_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_factory_mult value = 3 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = building_libra_yggdrasil_trade_1
		hidden_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_trade_mult value = 3 } }
		}
	}
	option = {

		hidden_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_physics_mult value = 3 } }

		hidden_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_engineering_mult value = 3 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = building_libra_yggdrasil_unity_1
		hidden_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_unity_mult value = 3 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = building_libra_yggdrasil_energy_1
		hidden_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = libra_yggdrasil_energy_mult value = 3 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = building_libra_yggdrasil_mineral_1
		hidden_effect = {

		}

	option = {

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18000
	title = libra_fairy.18000.name
	desc = libra_fairy.18000.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_heavenly_throne
	show_sound = {
		trigger = { is_robot_empire = yes }
		sound = event_psionic_robot
	}
	show_sound = {
		trigger = { is_robot_empire = no }
		sound = event_psionic
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	immediate = {
		every_owned_planet = {
			limit = { has_planetary_ascension_tier < 3 }
			set_planetary_ascension_tier = 3
		}
		while = {
			count = 5
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = { has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10 }
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18000.a
		custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.18000.tt
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18001
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 90 }
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_heavenly_throne
			target = capital_scope
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18005
	title = libra_fairy.18005.name
	desc = libra_fairy.18005.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_heavenly_throne
	show_sound = {
		trigger = { is_robot_empire = yes }
		sound = event_psionic_robot
	}
	show_sound = {
		trigger = { is_robot_empire = no }
		sound = event_psionic
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_1
		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_2
		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_3
		remove_modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_4
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18005.a
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_1
			multiplier = value:libra_heavenly_throne_agenda_mult
			days = 10800
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				libra_ai_preferences_technology = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18005.b
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_2
			multiplier = value:libra_heavenly_throne_agenda_mult
			days = 10800
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18005.c
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_3
			multiplier = value:libra_heavenly_throne_agenda_mult
			days = 10800
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				libra_ai_preferences_planet = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18005.d
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = agenda_libra_heavenly_throne_modifier_4
			multiplier = value:libra_heavenly_throne_agenda_mult
			days = 10800
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				or = {
					pop_amount < 80000
					libra_ai_preferences_population = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {

situation_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18011
	title = libra_fairy.18011.name
	desc = libra_fairy.18011.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_shroudwalker_enclave
	show_sound = {
		trigger = { owner = { is_robot_empire = yes } }
		sound = event_psionic_robot
	}
	show_sound = {
		trigger = { owner = { is_robot_empire = no } }
		sound = event_psionic
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	situation = this

	immediate = {
		set_situation_locked = yes
		owner = {
			change_variable = { which = libra_heavenly_throne_success value = 1 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18011.a
		custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.18011.a.tt
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				change_variable = { which = libra_heavenly_throne_1 value = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18011.b
		custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.18011.b.tt
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				change_variable = { which = libra_heavenly_throne_2 value = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18011.c
		custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.18011.c.tt
		hidden_effect = {
			owner = {
				change_variable = { which = libra_heavenly_throne_3 value = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				owner = { is_variable_set = libra_heavenly_throne_3 }
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				situation_progress >= 240
				situation_progress <= 360
			}
			custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.18011.tt.1
			hidden_effect = {
				owner = {
					random_owned_leader = {
						limit = {
							has_base_skill <= 9999
							has_trait = leader_trait_psionic
							nor = {
								has_trait = leader_trait_chosen
								has_trait = leader_trait_erudite
								has_trait = leader_trait_has_backup_clone
								has_trait = trait_leader_genetic_purist
								has_trait = leader_trait_cyborg
								has_trait = leader_trait_limited_cyborg
								has_trait = leader_trait_synthetic
								has_trait = leader_trait_virtual
							}
						}
						#remove_all_negative_traits = yes
						remove_trait = leader_trait_psionic
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_chosen show_message = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				situation_progress >= 120
				situation_progress <= 480
			}
			custom_tooltip = libra_fairy.18011.tt.2
			hidden_effect = {
				owner = {
					while = {
						count = 3
						random_owned_planet = {
							limit = { has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10 }
							set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			destroy_situation = this
			owner = {
				start_situation = { type = situation_libra_heavenly_throne target = capital_scope }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18020
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
		has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_heavenly_throne
		check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value > 0 }
		not = { has_country_flag = libra_fairy.18020_triggered }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_timed_country_flag = {
			flag = libra_fairy.18020_triggered
			days = 1080
		}
		random_list = {
			35 = {
				country_event = { id = libra_fairy.18021 days = 6 random = 30 }
			}
			24 = {
				country_event = { id = libra_fairy.18022 days = 6 random = 30 }
			}
			24 = {
				country_event = { id = libra_fairy.18023 days = 6 random = 30 }
			}
			13 = {
				country_event = { id = libra_fairy.18024 days = 6 random = 30 }
			}
			4 = {
				country_event = { id = libra_fairy.18025 days = 6 random = 30 }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18021
	title = libra_fairy.18021.name
	desc = libra_fairy.18021.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_shroudwalker_enclave
	show_sound = event_mystic_reveal

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable_to_random_value = { which = libra_hts_random min = -75 max = 75 rounded = yes }
		random_situation = {
			limit = { is_situation_type = situation_libra_heavenly_throne }
			add_situation_progress = root.libra_hts_random
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = GOOD
	}

	after = {
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = physics_research
			value = 6
			min = 120000
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = society_research
			value = 6
			min = 120000
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = engineering_research
			value = 6
			min = 120000
		}
		tooltip = {
			random_situation = {
				limit = { is_situation_type = situation_libra_heavenly_throne }
				add_situation_progress = root.libra_hts_random
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { clear_variable = libra_hts_random }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18100
	title = libra_fairy.18100.name
	desc = libra_fairy.18100.desc

	show_sound = {
		trigger = { is_robot_empire = yes }
		sound = event_psionic_robot
	}

		trigger = { is_robot_empire = no }
		sound = event_psionic
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = libra_fairy.18100.a
		add_research_option = tech_libra_psionic_destiny
		add_research_option = tech_libra_psionic_favour
		add_research_option = tech_libra_psionic_reflection

			modifier = libra_liber_genesis_mod
			days = 5400
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18101
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 91 }
		country_event = { id = libra_fairy.18102 days = 720 }
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				has_base_skill <= 9999
				can_have_libra_special_ruler_traits = yes
				or = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_psionic
					has_trait = leader_trait_chosen
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_liber_genesis show_message = no }
			}
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				not = { has_trait = leader_trait_libra_liber_genesis }
				can_have_libra_special_ruler_traits = yes
				has_base_skill <= 9999
				or = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_psionic
					has_trait = leader_trait_chosen
				}
			}
			add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_liber_genesis show_message = yes }
		}
		create_message = {
			type = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT
			localization = MESSAGE_ALL_GAINED_TRAIT_DESC
			days = @toast_message_days
			variable = {
				type = key
				value = leader_trait_libra_liber_genesis
				localization = TRAIT
			}
			variable = {
				key = TRAIT_KEY
				value = leader_trait_libra_liber_genesis
			}
			variable = {
				key = "border"
				value = "GFX_invisible"
			}
		}
	}
}
