country_event = {
	id = libra_fairy.18900
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_government_cooldown = no
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_buildings = {
					type = building_libra_psionic_destiny_sanctum
					value = 3
				}
			}
			while = {
				count = value:libra_psionic_eulogy_ascension
				random_owned_planet = {
					limit = { has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10 }
					set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_buildings = {
					type = building_libra_psionic_favour_sanctum
					value = 3
				}
			}
			capital_scope = {
				create_pop_group = {
					species = owner_main_species
					size = root.value:libra_psionic_eulogy_pop
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_buildings = {
					type = building_libra_psionic_reflection_sanctum
					value = 3
				}
			}
			while = {
				count = value:libra_psionic_eulogy_skill
				random_owned_leader = {
					limit = {
						has_base_skill < 10
						can_have_libra_special_councilor_traits = yes
					}
					add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

namespace = libra_main

event = {
	id = libra_main.0000
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_rim_system = {
			spawn_system = {
				min_distance >= 60
				max_distance <= 70
				direction = rimwards
				initializer = libra_story_site_system_2
				hyperlane = no
			}
		}
		random_system = {
			limit = { is_rim_system = no }
			spawn_system = {
				min_distance >= 20
				max_distance <= 30
				direction = rimwards
				initializer = libra_event_system_1
			}
			weights = {
				base = 10
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					has_star_flag = empire_cluster
				}
			}
		}
		every_playable_country = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			country_event = { id = libra_main.0001 days = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						any_playable_country = {
							is_ai = no
							has_country_flag = libra_game_host
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = libra_game_host
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_main.0011
	title = "libra_main.0011.name"
	desc = "libra_main.0011.desc"
	show_sound = par_event_beholder

	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "enclave_artist_window"
	custom_gui_option = "enclave_artist_option"

	trigger = {
		has_multiple_ascension_path = no
		num_ascension_perks > 3

	}

			}

				set_government_cooldown = no
				add_research_option = tech_libra_psionic_apocalypse
			}
			tr_virtuality_finish = {
				add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = 500 }
				add_research_option = tech_libra_reality_deconstruction
			}
			tr_nanotech_finish = {
				add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = 200 }
				add_research_option = tech_libra_nanite_shipyard
				add_research_option = tech_libra_nanite_locusts

			tr_modularity_finish = {
				set_government_cooldown = no
				add_research_option = tech_libra_modular_society
				add_research_option = tech_mine_living_metal
			}
			tr_synthetics_finish = {
				add_research_option = tech_libra_synthetic_commands
				add_research_option = tech_libra_synthetic_leaders
			}
			tr_cybernetics_assimilator_finish = {
				add_research_option = tech_libra_centralised_cybernetics
				add_research_option = tech_libra_cybernetic_growth_1
			}

				add_research_option = tech_libra_centralised_cybernetics

				add_research_option = tech_libra_mutation_encoding
			}
			tr_purity_finish = {
				add_research_option = tech_libra_genetics_demystified
				add_research_option = tech_libra_purity_sanctuary
			}
			tr_cloning_finish = {
				add_research_option = tech_libra_genetics_demystified
				add_research_option = tech_libra_cloning_method
			}
			tr_genetics_finish = {
				add_research_option = tech_libra_genetics_demystified
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = unity value = 6 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable_arithmetic = {
					which = value:libra_fe_assembly_clinic_count
					value > 24
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = libra_disable_rat
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_main.0011.a
		custom_tooltip = libra_main.0011.a.tt
	}

		name = libra_main.0011.b

				amount = 25

		}

country_event = {
	id = libra_main.0106
	title = "libra_main.0106.name"
	desc = "libra_main.0106.desc"
	show_sound = par_event_beholder

	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = "enclave_artist_window"
	custom_gui_option = "enclave_artist_option"

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = event_target:libra_agent
		room = libra_hoshino_kotori_01_room
	}

	immediate = {
		ordered_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				leader_class = commander
				is_hidden = no
				is_event_leader = no
				is_leader_tier = leader_tier_default
			}
			position = 0
			order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
			inverse = yes
			save_event_target_as = leader_1
		}
		ordered_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				leader_class = commander
				is_hidden = no
				is_event_leader = no
				is_leader_tier = leader_tier_default
				not = {
					is_same_value = event_target:leader_1
				}
			}
			position = 0
			order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
			inverse = yes
			save_event_target_as = leader_2
		}
		ordered_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				leader_class = commander
				is_hidden = no
				is_event_leader = no
				is_leader_tier = leader_tier_default
				nor = {
					is_same_value = event_target:leader_1
					is_same_value = event_target:leader_2
				}
			}
			position = 0
			order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
			inverse = yes
			save_event_target_as = leader_3
		}
		ordered_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				leader_class = commander
				is_hidden = no
				is_event_leader = no
				is_leader_tier = leader_tier_default
				nor = {
					is_same_value = event_target:leader_1
					is_same_value = event_target:leader_2
					is_same_value = event_target:leader_3
				}
			}
			position = 0
			order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
			inverse = yes
			save_event_target_as = leader_4
		}
	}

	after = {
		name = libra_main.0106.a
		hidden_effect = { remove_country_flag = libra_agent_event }
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_main.0106.a
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_main.0106.b
		response_text = libra_main.0106.response
		trigger = {
			exists = event_target:leader_1
		}
		event_target:leader_1 = {
			add_trait = {
				trait = leader_trait_libra_reciprocity_commander
				show_message = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_main.0106.c
		response_text = libra_main.0106.response
		trigger = {
			exists = event_target:leader_2
		}
		event_target:leader_2 = {
			add_trait = {
				trait = leader_trait_libra_reciprocity_commander
				show_message = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_main.0106.d
		response_text = libra_main.0106.response
		trigger = {
			exists = event_target:leader_3
		}
		event_target:leader_3 = {
			add_trait = {
				trait = leader_trait_libra_reciprocity_commander
				show_message = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_main.0106.e
		response_text = libra_main.0106.response
		trigger = {
			exists = event_target:leader_4
		}

	desc = "libra_main.2024.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_crystal_mining
	show_sound = event_finding_loot
	location = from

	is_triggered_only = yes

	archaeology = yes

	immediate = {
		from = { set_site_progress_locked = yes }
	}

	after = {
		from = { set_site_progress_locked = no }
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_main.2024.a
		owner = {
			libra_main_story_site_effect_1 = yes
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = unity value = 3 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_main.2024.b
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = { has_technology = tech_libra_analytics }
				}
				add_tech_progress = { tech = tech_libra_analytics progress = 0.25 }
			}
			else = {
				add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = physics_research value = 3 }
			}
			add_monthly_resource_mult = { resource = engineering_research value = 3 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

			}

			}

					override_texture = { "background:GFX_origin_libra_extreme_iteration" }
					override_id = timeline_origin_libra_extreme_iteration
				}
			}
			origin_libra_centurion = {

				trait = leader_trait_libra_ceremony
				show_message = no
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = libra_praises_to_life_count value = 0 }
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_praises_to_life_b
			target = capital_scope
			effect = {
				add_situation_progress = 36
				set_timed_situation_flag = {
					flag = libra_situation_event
					days = 15
				}
				#situation_event = { id = libra_origin.0102 days = 21 random = 1 }
			}
		}
		start_situation = {
			type = situation_libra_praises_to_life_a
			target = capital_scope
			effect = {
				add_situation_progress = 10
				set_situation_approach = situation_libra_praises_to_life_a_a
				remove_situation_flag = path_locked
				situation_event = { id = libra_origin.0104 days = 19 random = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_gestalt = no
				num_council_positions < 5
			}
			unlock_council_slots = 1
			set_council_position_to_council = councilor_libra_praises_to_life
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			add_modifier = { modifier = libra_origin_gestalt_modifier }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0103
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_variable_set = libra_praises_to_life_count
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_praises_to_life_count
					value > 0
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				which = libra_praises_to_life_research_count
				value = libra_praises_to_life_count
			}
			remove_modifier = agenda_libra_praises_to_life_modifier_1
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = agenda_libra_praises_to_life_modifier_1
				multiplier = libra_praises_to_life_research_count
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = libra_praises_to_life_count
						value < 340
					}
				}
				remove_modifier = agenda_libra_praises_to_life_modifier_2
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = agenda_libra_praises_to_life_modifier_2
					multiplier = libra_praises_to_life_count
				}
			}
			else = {
				remove_modifier = agenda_libra_praises_to_life_modifier_2
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = agenda_libra_praises_to_life_modifier_2
					multiplier = 340
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

			switch = {
				trigger = leader_class

					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_scientist
						show_message = no
					}
				}
				official = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_official
						show_message = no
					}
				}
				commander = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_commander
						show_message = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				nor = {
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_0
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_1
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_2
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_3
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_4
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_5
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_6
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_7
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_8
					has_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_9
				}
			}
			modify_species = {
				species = this
				add_trait = trait_libra_psalm_of_waves_0
				add_traits_at_start_of_list = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
				every_owned_leader = {
					limit = { is_councilor = yes }
					set_gestalt_node_portrait_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_gestalt = no
				num_council_positions < 5
			}
			unlock_council_slots = 1
			set_council_position_to_council = councilor_libra_psalm_of_waves
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			add_modifier = { modifier = libra_origin_gestalt_modifier }
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0400
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_enlightenment_wisdom
		not = { has_ethic = ethic_gestalt_consciousness }
	}

	immediate = {
		while = {
			count = 2
			if = {
				limit = { 
					count_owned_leader = {
						count < 3
						limit = { leader_class = official }
					}
				}
				random_pool_leader = {
					limit = { leader_class = official }
					root = {
						clone_leader = { target = prev }
					}
					kill_leader = { show_notification = no }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					count_owned_leader = {
						count < 3
						limit = { leader_class = commander }
					}
				}
				random_pool_leader = {
					limit = { leader_class = commander }
					root = {
						clone_leader = { target = prev }
					}
					kill_leader = { show_notification = no }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					count_owned_leader = {
						count < 3
						limit = { leader_class = scientist }
					}
				}
				random_pool_leader = {
					limit = { leader_class = scientist }
					root = {
						clone_leader = { target = prev }
					}
					kill_leader = { show_notification = no }
				}
			}
		}
		every_pool_leader = {
			kill_leader = { show_notification = no }
		}
		remove_modifier = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_start_modifier
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_start_modifier
			multiplier = -50
		}
		set_country_flag = libra_forbid_leader_pool
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_hidden = no
				is_ruler = no
				leader_class = official
			}
			set_skill = 10
			libra_legendary_leader_effect = yes
			add_trait = { trait = random_common show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			set_variable_to_random_value = { which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age min = 1300 max = 1600 rounded = yes }
			add_age = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_hidden = no
				is_ruler = no
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_skill = 10
			libra_legendary_leader_effect = yes
			add_trait = { trait = random_common show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			set_variable_to_random_value = { which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age min = 1300 max = 1600 rounded = yes }
			add_age = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age
		}
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_hidden = no
				is_ruler = no
				leader_class = scientist
			}
			set_skill = 10
			libra_legendary_leader_effect = yes
			set_leader_flag = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_effect
			add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_wisdom_scientist show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			add_trait = { trait = subclass_scientist_explorer show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_slippery_2 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_prospector_2 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_observant_2 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_tuner_2 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			add_trait = { trait = random_common show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
			set_variable_to_random_value = { which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age min = 2400 max = 3000 rounded = yes }
			add_age = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age
			clear_variable = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age
			weights = {
				base = 10
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					is_councilor = yes	
					root = {
						any_owned_leader = {
							leader_class = scientist
							is_councilor = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_hidden = no
				not = { has_leader_flag = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_effect }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_base_skill < 5 }
				set_skill = 5
			}
			set_variable_to_random_value = { which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age min = 700 max = 1400 rounded = yes }
			add_age = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age
			clear_variable = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_age
			if = {

					not = { has_leader_flag = renowned_leader }
				}

			}
			switch = {
				trigger = leader_class
				official = {

						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_reformer_3 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_cartographer_3 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
					}
					else = {
						add_trait = { trait = subclass_official_governor show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_settler_3 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_pop_focused_3 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_distribution_lines_3 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
					}
				}
				commander = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_wisdom_commander show_message = no consume_selection = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { is_councilor = yes }

						add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_armada_logistician_2 show_message = no consume_selection = yes }

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0401
	title = "libra_origin.0401.name"
	desc = {
		text = libra_origin.0401.a.desc
		trigger = {
			exists = heir
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = libra_origin.0401.b.desc
		trigger = {
			not = { exists = heir }
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_enclave_overlord
	show_sound = event_coronation
	event_window_type = leader_story
	event_picture_background = "GFX_leader_recruitment_bg_renowned"
	is_triggered_only = yes

	picture_event_data = {
		portrait = event_target:libra_enlightenment_wisdom_leader
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = heir
			}
			heir = {
				add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1
				save_event_target_as = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_leader
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_leader = {
				name = random
				species = root
				skill = 1
				class = commander
				background_job = noble
				effect = {
					add_trait = { trait = random_common show_message = no consume_selection = no }
					add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1
					save_event_target_as = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_leader
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:libra_enlightenment_wisdom_leader = {
			switch = {
				trigger = leader_class
				official = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_official show_message = yes consume_selection = no }
				}
				commander = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_commander show_message = yes consume_selection = no }
				}
				scientist = {
					add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_scientist show_message = yes consume_selection = no }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0401.a
		custom_tooltip = libra_origin.0401.a.tt
	}
}

	desc = "libra_origin.0402.desc"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = { is_variable_set = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_mult }
			set_variable_to_random_value = {
				which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_random
				min = 4
				max = 12
				rounded = yes
			}
			subtract_variable = {
				which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_mult
				value = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_random

			clear_variable = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_random
			if = {
				limit = { not = { is_leader_tier = leader_tier_legendary } }

					chance = 20
					add_trait = { trait = random_negative }
				}
			}
		}
	}

leader_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0410
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = owner
		owner = {
			or = {
				years_passed < 32
				not = { has_origin = origin_libra_enlightenment_wisdom }
				count_owned_leader = {
					count > 3
					limit = { is_variable_set = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_mult }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner = { not = { has_origin = origin_libra_enlightenment_wisdom } }
			}
			kill_leader = { show_notification = yes }
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0411 }
				}
				1 = {
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0412 }
				}
				1 = {
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0413 }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 2
						leader_class = official
					}
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0414 }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 2
						leader_class = official
					}
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0415 }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 2
						leader_class = commander
					}
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0416 }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 2
						leader_class = commander
					}
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0417 }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 2
						leader_class = scientist
					}
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0418 }
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 2
						leader_class = scientist
					}
					leader_event = { id = libra_origin.0419 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0445
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_enlightenment_wisdom
	}

	immediate = {
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.0445 days = 480 random = 240 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_planet = { has_planet_flag = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_cap }
			}
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
				set_variable_to_random_value = {
					which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_exp
					min = 18
					max = 36
					rounded = yes
				}
				multiply_variable = {
					which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_exp
					value = trigger:has_base_skill
				}
				set_variable_to_random_value = {
					which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_random
					min = 36
					max = 108
					rounded = yes
				}
				change_variable = {
					which = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_exp
					value = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_random
				}
				add_experience = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_exp
				clear_variable = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_exp
				clear_variable = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_random
			}
			random = {
				chance = 35
				random_owned_leader = {
					limit = {
						is_same_species = root
						nor = {
							is_variable_set = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_mult
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_official
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_commander
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_scientist
						}
					}
					switch = {
						trigger = leader_class
						official = {
							add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_official show_message = yes consume_selection = no }
						}
						commander = {
							add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_commander show_message = yes consume_selection = no }
						}
						scientist = {
							add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_libra_enlightenment_heritage_scientist show_message = yes consume_selection = no }
						}
					}
					weights = {
						base = 1
						modifier = {
							factor = 2
							has_total_skill > 4
						}
						modifier = {
							factor = 2
							has_total_skill > 8
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0450.a
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = libra_enlightenment_wisdom_mod_1
		}
	}
}

# crimson nights

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0500
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_crimson_nights
		is_species_class = HUM
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = {
			switch = {
				trigger = leader_class
				official = {
					remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_official
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_official
						show_message = no
					}
				}
				commander = {
					remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_commander
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_commander
						show_message = no
					}
				}
				scientist = {
					remove_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_scientist
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_scientist
						show_message = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_crimson_reap_total
			value = 650
		}
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_crimson_reap_count
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_crimson_reap_subject
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_awaken_count
			value = 1
		}
		capital_scope = {
			add_building = building_libra_crimson_nights
			add_building = building_foundry_1
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_farming }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_gestalt = no
			}
			set_policy = {
				policy = robot_pop_policy
				option = robot_pops_allowed
				cooldown = no
			}
			if = {
				limit = { num_council_positions < 5 }
				unlock_council_slots = 1
				set_council_position_to_council = councilor_libra_crimson_nights
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_leader = {
						is_councilor = no
						or = {
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_commander
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_official
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_scientist
						}
					}
				}
				random_owned_leader = { 
					limit = {
						is_councilor = no
						or = {
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_commander
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_official
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_scientist
						}
					}
					set_council_position = councilor_libra_crimson_nights
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_pool_leader = {
					limit = {
						leader_class = commander
						or = {
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_commander
							has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire_official
						}
					}
					root = {
						clone_leader = {
							target = prev
							effect = { set_council_position = councilor_libra_crimson_nights }
						}
					}
					kill_leader = { show_notification = no }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			add_modifier = { modifier = libra_origin_gestalt_modifier }
		}
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.0511 days = 10 }
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0501
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		owner = {
			has_origin = origin_libra_crimson_nights
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_modifier = libra_crimson_nights_planet_sideeffect }
			export_modifier_duration_to_variable = {
				modifier = libra_crimson_nights_planet_sideeffect
				variable = libra_crimson_nights_sideeffect_time_mult
			}
			remove_modifier = libra_crimson_nights_planet_sideeffect
		}
		change_variable = {
			which = libra_crimson_nights_sideeffect_time_mult
			value = 1800
		}
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = libra_crimson_nights_planet_sideeffect
			days = 1
			time_multiplier = libra_crimson_nights_sideeffect_time_mult
		}
		clear_variable = libra_crimson_nights_sideeffect_time_mult
	}
}

	id = libra_origin.0502
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		not = { has_modifier = libra_crimson_nights_planet_sideeffect }
		From = {
			has_origin = origin_libra_crimson_nights
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = libra_crimson_nights_planet_sideeffect
			days = 5400
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots > 0
				From = {
					is_ai = yes
					num_buildings = {
						type = building_libra_crimson_nights
						value < 11
					}
				}
				not = { has_building = building_libra_crimson_nights }
			}
			add_building = building_libra_crimson_nights
		}
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0503
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_timed_planet_flag = {
			flag = recently_decision_crimson_vampire
			days = 3600
		}
		export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
			trigger = num_uncleared_blockers
			variable = libra_crimson_reap_planet_count
		}
		clear_blockers = yes
		multiply_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_planet_count value = 200 }
		export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
			trigger = count_owned_pop_amount
			parameters = {
				limit = {
					nor = {
						is_same_species = root
						is_pop_category = pre_sapients
					}
				}
			}
			variable = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count
		}
		owner = {
			change_variable = { which = libra_pop_purged_count value = root.libra_crimson_reap_pops_count }
		}
		change_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_planet_count value = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count }
		if = {
			limit = { owner = { has_tradition = tr_libra_crimson_ancestor_adopt } }
			multiply_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count value = 2 }
		}
		owner = {
			add_resource = {
				unity = 1
				sr_dark_matter = 0.05
				mult = root.libra_crimson_reap_planet_count
			}
			change_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_total value = root.libra_crimson_reap_planet_count }
			change_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value = root.libra_crimson_reap_planet_count }
			while = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value > 6000 } }
				subtract_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value = 6000 }
				libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_awaken_effect = yes
			}
		}
		divide_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_planet_count value = 4 }
		floor_variable = libra_crimson_reap_planet_count
		create_pop_group = {
			species = owner_main_species
			size = libra_crimson_reap_planet_count
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				nor = {
					is_same_species = root
					is_pop_category = pre_sapients
				}
			}
			kill_pop_group = {
				pop_group = this
				percentage = 1
			}
		}
		clear_variable = libra_crimson_reap_planet_count
		clear_variable = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0504
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = unity
			value = 13
		}
		add_monthly_resource_mult = {
			resource = sr_dark_matter
			value = 13
		}
		libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_awaken_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_awaken_count value < 50 }
			}
			capital_scope = {
				while = {
					count = root.libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_awaken_count
					create_pop_group = { species = root }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			capital_scope = {
				while = {
					count = 50
					create_pop = { species = root }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					has_trait = leader_trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_official

					has_trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire
				}
				exists = planet

				}

			mult = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count
		}

		change_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_total value = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count }
		change_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count }
		while = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value > 6000 } }
			subtract_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value = 6000 }

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0507
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
			trigger = sapient_pop_amount
			variable = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count
		}
		divide_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count value = 10 }
		floor_variable = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				is_sapient = yes
				pop_group_size > 2
			}
			kill_pop_group = {
				pop_group = this
				percentage = 0.1
			}
		}
		add_resource = {
			unity = 2
			sr_dark_matter = 0.1
			mult = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count
		}
		overlord = {
			change_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_total value = root.libra_crimson_reap_pops_count }
			change_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value = root.libra_crimson_reap_pops_count }
			while = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value > 6000 } }
				subtract_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_count value = 6000 }
				libra_crimson_nights_ancestor_awaken_effect = yes
			}
			set_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_subject value = root.libra_crimson_reap_pops_count }
			divide_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_subject value = 2 }
			floor_variable = libra_crimson_reap_subject
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = libra_crimson_reap_subject value > 0 } }
				capital_scope = {
					create_pop_group = {
						species = owner_main_species
						size = libra_crimson_reap_subject
					}
				}
			}
			clear_variable = libra_crimson_reap_subject
		}
		clear_variable = libra_crimson_reap_pops_count
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0540
	title = "libra_origin.0540.name"
	picture = GFX_evt_libra_crimson_nights_2
	show_sound = event_the_great_awakening
	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt
		}
		text = libra_origin.0540.desc.a
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			not = { has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt }
		}
		text = libra_origin.0540.desc.b
	}

	trigger = {
		or = {
			is_species_class = HUM
			is_species_class = ROBOT
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = synth_virtual }
			owner_main_species = {
				create_species = {
					is_mod = yes
					name = this
					plural = this
					class = "ROBOT"
					portrait = this
					can_be_modified = this
					namelist = this
					gender = this
					homeworld = this
					extra_trait_points = 1
					traits = {
						trait = trait_mechanical
						trait = trait_robot_digital_1
						trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor
					}
				}
			}
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					species = { is_species_class = ROBOT }
					not = { has_trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor }
				}
				change_species = last_created_species
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt }
			owner_main_species = {
				create_species = {
					is_mod = yes
					name = this
					plural = this
					class = "ROBOT"
					portrait = this
					can_be_modified = this
					namelist = this
					gender = this
					homeworld = this
					extra_trait_points = 1
					traits = {
						trait = trait_mechanical
						trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor
					}
				}
			}
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					species = { is_species_class = ROBOT }
					not = { has_trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor }
				}
				change_species = last_created_species
			}
		}
		else = {
			modify_species = {
				species = owner_main_species
				add_trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor
				remove_trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_vampire
			}
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				species = { is_species_class = HUM }
				not = { has_trait = trait_libra_crimson_nights_ancestor }
			}
			change_species = last_created_species
		}
		change_dominant_species = { species = last_created_species }
		capital_scope = {
			create_pop_group = { species = last_created_species }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = { is_councilor = yes }
				set_gestalt_node_portrait_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		custom_tooltip = libra_origin.0540.tooltip
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0540.a
		trigger = {
			has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0540.b
		trigger = {
			has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0540.c
		trigger = {
			has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt
			has_valid_civic = civic_aristocratic_elite
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0540.d
		trigger = {
			not = { has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0540.e
		trigger = {
			not = { has_tradition = tr_synthetics_adopt }
			has_valid_civic = civic_aristocratic_elite
		}
	}
}

# shadow consciousness

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0600
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_shadow_consciousness
		has_authority = auth_hive_mind
	}

	immediate = {
		give_technology = { tech = tech_mine_dark_matter message = no }
		set_country_flag = dark_matter_found
		if = {
			limit = { is_lithoid_empire = no }
			add_resource = { energy = 100 food = 100 sr_dark_matter = 100 }
		}
		else = {
			add_resource = { energy = 100 minerals = 100 sr_dark_matter = 100 }
		}
		capital_scope = {
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_valid_civic = civic_libra_shadow_consciousness_a }
			change_variable = { which = libra_shadow_consciousness_count value = 1 }
			capital_scope = {
				create_pop_group = { species = root size = 350 }
				add_building = {
					zone = zone_foundry
					building = building_foundry_1
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_valid_civic = civic_libra_shadow_consciousness_b }
			change_variable = { which = libra_shadow_consciousness_count value = 1 }
			capital_scope = {
				create_pop_group = { species = root size = 350 }
				add_building = {
					zone = zone_research_unity
					building = building_hive_node
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_valid_civic = civic_libra_shadow_consciousness_c }
			change_variable = { which = libra_shadow_consciousness_count value = 1 }
			capital_scope = {
				create_pop_group = { species = root size = 350 }
				add_building = building_research_lab_1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_valid_civic = civic_libra_shadow_consciousness_d }
			change_variable = { which = libra_shadow_consciousness_count value = 1 }
			capital_scope = {
				create_pop_group = { species = root size = 350 }
				add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_variable_set = libra_shadow_consciousness_count }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = libra_shadow_consciousness_count
						value > 3
					}
				}
				add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = -100 }
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = libra_shadow_consciousness_negative_modifier
					multiplier = 10
					days = 7200
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = libra_shadow_consciousness_count
						value > 2
					}
				}
				add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = -50 }
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = libra_shadow_consciousness_negative_modifier
					multiplier = 6
					days = 7200
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = libra_shadow_consciousness_count
						value > 1
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = libra_shadow_consciousness_negative_modifier
					multiplier = 3
					days = 7200
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = {
					modifier = libra_shadow_consciousness_negative_modifier
					multiplier = 1
					days = 7200
				}
			}
			clear_variable = libra_shadow_consciousness_count
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0610
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = {
			resource = sr_dark_matter
			variable = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			which = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
			value = 0.75
		}
		add_resource = {
			sr_dark_matter = -1
			mult = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
		}
		set_variable_to_random_value = {
			which = libra_shadow_consciousness_random
			min = 5
			max = 6
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			which = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
			value = libra_shadow_consciousness_random
		}
		add_resource = {
			alloys = 1
			mult = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
		}
		clear_variable = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
		clear_variable = libra_shadow_consciousness_random
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0620
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = {
			resource = sr_dark_matter
			variable = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
		}
		divide_variable = {
			which = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
			value = 50
		}
		floor_variable = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
					value > 1
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				which = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
				value = 30
			}
		}
		while = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_leader = { has_base_skill < 10 }
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
					value > 0
				}
			}
			add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = -50 }
			subtract_variable = {
				which = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
				value = 1
			}
			ordered_owned_leader = {
				limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
				position = 0
				order_by = trigger:has_base_skill
				inverse = yes
				add_experience = 2500
			}
		}
		clear_variable = libra_shadow_consciousness_darkmatter
	}
}

#

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0700
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_dragonborn
		is_species_class = REP
	}

	immediate = {
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = origin_libra_dragonborn_effect
			days = 3600
		}
		species = {
			set_species_flag = libra_dragonborn_species
		}
		ruler = {
			add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1
		}
		while = {
			count = 2
			random_pool_leader = {
				limit = { leader_class = official }
				root = {
					clone_leader = {
						target = prev
						effect = { add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1 }
					}
				}
				kill_leader = {
					show_notification = no
				}
			}
			random_pool_leader = {
				limit = { leader_class = commander }
				root = {
					clone_leader = {
						target = prev
						effect = { add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1 }
					}
				}
				kill_leader = {
					show_notification = no
				}
			}
			random_pool_leader = {
				limit = { leader_class = scientist }
				root = {
					clone_leader = {
						target = prev
						effect = { add_skill_without_trait_selection = 1 }
					}
				}
				kill_leader = {
					show_notification = no
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			random_owned_fleet = {
				limit = { is_ship_class = shipclass_military }
				destroy_fleet = {
					target = this
					kill_leader = no
					destroy_template = yes
				}
			}
		}
		capital_scope = {
			add_deposit = d_searing_desert
			add_deposit = d_ore_rich_caverns
			add_deposit = d_fungal_caves
			add_deposit = d_libra_dragonborn_home
			add_district = {
				district_type = district_generator
				ignore_cap = yes
				type_conversion = yes
			}
			add_district = {
				district_type = district_generator
				ignore_cap = yes
				type_conversion = yes
			}
			add_district = {
				district_type = district_farming
				ignore_cap = yes
				type_conversion = yes
			}
			while = {
				count = 3
				add_district = {
					district_type = district_mining
					ignore_cap = yes
					type_conversion = yes
				}
				add_district = {
					district_type = district_farming
					ignore_cap = yes
					type_conversion = yes
				}
			}
			every_deposit = {
				limit = { is_deposit_type = d_libra_dragonborn_home }
				set_deposit_flag = keep_deposit_on_terraform
			}
			if = {
				limit = { root = { is_ai = no } }
				solar_system = {
					random_system_planet = {
						limit = { has_planet_flag = libra_dragonborn_arc_site }
						remove_planet_flag = libra_dragonborn_arc_site
						create_archaeological_site = arc_site_libra_dragonborn
						root = { set_country_flag = libra_dragonborn_site_owner_@prev }
					}
				}
			}
			while = {
				count = 2
				root = {
					random_owned_leader = {
						limit = {
							leader_class = commander
							is_councilor = no
							is_idle = yes
						}
						save_event_target_as = libra_leader_commander_to_set
					}
				}
				create_fleet = {
					effect = {
						set_owner = root
						create_ship = {
							name = NAME_Libra_Dragonborn_Inital
							design = NAME_Libra_Dragonborn_Inital
							prefix = no
							effect = {
								add_modifier = {
									modifier = origin_libra_dragonborn_ship_effect
									days = 3600
								}
							}
						}
						assign_leader = event_target:libra_leader_commander_to_set
						set_location = root.capital_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		while = {
			count = 24
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = { pop_group_size > 50 }
				kill_pop_group = {
					pop_group = this
					amount = 50
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_gestalt = no
				num_council_positions < 5
			}
			unlock_council_slots = 1
			set_council_position_to_council = councilor_libra_dragonborn
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = no }
				random_owned_leader = {
					limit = { is_councilor = no }
					set_council_position = councilor_libra_dragonborn
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			add_modifier = { modifier = libra_origin_gestalt_modifier }
		}
		if = {	#AI没有遗址，研发科技
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			add_resource = { sr_living_metal = 3 }
			add_tech_progress = { tech = tech_libra_dragon_breath_1 progress = 0.85 }
		}
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_dragonborn_decision_count
			value = 1
		}
	}
}

			remove_species_flag = libra_dragonborn_species
			set_species_flag = trait_libra_dragonborn_upgraded
		}
		modify_species = {
			species = owner_main_species
			remove_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_0
			add_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_6
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				species = { is_species_class = REP }
				not = { has_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_6 }
			}
			change_species = last_created_species
		}
		change_dominant_species = { species = last_created_species }
		capital_scope = {
			create_pop_group = { species = last_created_species }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = { is_councilor = yes }
				set_gestalt_node_portrait_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = MARVELOUS
		custom_tooltip = libra_origin.0730.tooltip
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0731
	title = "libra_origin.0731.name"
	desc = "libra_origin.0731.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_psionics
	show_sound = event_the_great_awakening

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = libra_exdimensional_fusion_a }
			species = {
				remove_species_flag = libra_dragonborn_species
				set_species_flag = trait_libra_dragonborn_upgraded
			}
			modify_species = {
				species = owner_main_species
				remove_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_0
				add_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_5
			}
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					species = {
						or = {
							is_species_class = EXD
							is_species_class = LIBRAEXD
						}
					}
					not = { has_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_5 }
				}
				change_species = last_created_species
			}
		}
		else = {
			species = {
				remove_species_flag = libra_dragonborn_species
				set_species_flag = trait_libra_dragonborn_upgraded
			}
			modify_species = {
				species = owner_main_species
				remove_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_0
				add_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_4
			}
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					species = { is_species_class = REP }
					not = { has_trait = trait_libra_dragonborn_4 }
				}
				change_species = last_created_species
			}
		}
		change_dominant_species = { species = last_created_species }
		capital_scope = {
			create_pop_group = { species = last_created_species }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = MARVELOUS
		if = {
			limit  = { has_country_flag = libra_exdimensional_fusion_a }
			custom_tooltip = libra_origin.0731.b.tooltip
		}
		else = { custom_tooltip = libra_origin.0731.a.tooltip }
	}
}

		add_research_option = tech_libra_dragonborn_shield
		add_research_option = tech_libra_dragonborn_armor
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 4
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = libra_origin.0739.e
		allow = {
			resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = influence value < 150 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

# paradox loop

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0800
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_paradox_loop
		has_authority = auth_machine_intelligence
	}

	immediate = {
		add_research_option = tech_mineral_purification_1
		give_technology = { tech = tech_mine_betharian message = no }
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = { is_councilor_type = councilor_gestalt_cognitive }
			switch = {
				trigger = has_trait
				default = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_maniacal
						show_message = no
					}
				}
				leader_trait_maniacal = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_maniacal_2
						show_message = no
					}
				}
				leader_trait_maniacal_2 = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = leader_trait_maniacal_3
						show_message = no
					}
				}
				leader_trait_maniacal_3 = {
					add_trait = {
						trait = random_common
						show_message = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
		capital_scope = {
			add_deposit = d_betharian_deposit
			while = {
				limit = { has_district = district_generator }
				remove_district = district_generator
				add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
					district = district_mining
				}
			}
			while = {
				limit = { has_district = district_geothermal }
				remove_district = district_geothermal
				add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
					district = district_melting
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_district = district_melting }
				add_zone = {
					district = district_melting
					zone = zone_betharian
					zone_slot = 1
					replace = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_zone = {
					district = district_mining
					zone = zone_betharian
					zone_slot = 1
					replace = yes
				}
			}
			add_building = {
				zone = zone_betharian
				building = building_libra_paradox_processor
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			add_resource = { minerals = -200 }
			capital_scope = { add_building = building_betharian_power_plant }
		}
	}
}

# paradise lose

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.0900
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_paradise_lose
		has_civic = civic_dystopian_society
	}

	immediate = {
		give_technology = { tech = tech_penal_colonies message = no }
		add_tech_progress = { tech = tech_neural_implants progress = 0.25 }
		capital_scope = {
			remove_building = building_precinct_house
			add_building = building_libra_paradise_lose
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
				district = district_city
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = root
				size = 800
			}
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			add_trait = {
				trait = leader_trait_libra_paradise_lose
				show_message = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_council_positions < 5
			}
			unlock_council_slots = 1
			set_council_position_to_council = councilor_dystopian_society
		}
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.0908 days = 1775 }
	}
}

		}
	}

			show_message = yes
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1000
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_interstellar_port
		has_authority = auth_corporate
	}

	immediate = {
		capital_scope = {
			set_timed_planet_flag = {
				flag = ignore_ai_building_limitations
				days = 1800
			}
			add_building = building_holo_theatres
			add_building = building_libra_interstellar_exchange
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_city }
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_city }
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_city }
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
			if = {
				limit = { has_district = district_farming }
				add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_farming }
			}
			else = {
				add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
			}
			create_pop_group = {
				species = root
				size = 600
			}
			change_planet_size = 3
			add_deposit = d_libra_feature_17
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_total_civic_points = 2
				calc_true_if = {
					amount = 2
					has_civic = civic_libra_interstellar_port_a
					has_civic = civic_libra_interstellar_port_b
					has_civic = civic_libra_interstellar_port_c
					has_civic = civic_libra_interstellar_port_d
					has_civic = civic_libra_interstellar_port_e
					has_civic = civic_libra_interstellar_port_f
				}
			}
			add_research_option = tech_libra_interstellar_logistics_planning
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			add_resource = { alloys = 500 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_corporate_hedonism }
				force_remove_civic = civic_corporate_hedonism
				set_government_cooldown = no
				add_resource = { unity = 750 }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1105
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_ai = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_machine_beastmasters }
				force_remove_civic = civic_machine_beastmasters
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_machine_maintenance_protocols }
				force_remove_civic = civic_machine_maintenance_protocols
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_machine_diplomatic_protocols }
				force_remove_civic = civic_machine_diplomatic_protocols
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_machine_catalytic_processing }
				force_remove_civic = civic_machine_catalytic_processing
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_machine_guided_sapience }
				random = {
					chance = 50
					force_remove_civic = civic_machine_guided_sapience
					set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = libra_civic_removed }
				set_government_cooldown = no
				add_resource = { unity = 750 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						nor = {
							has_civic = civic_machine_servitor
							has_civic = civic_machine_assimilator
							has_civic = civic_machine_memorialist
							has_civic = civic_machine_exploration_protocol
							has_civic = civic_machine_sovereign_guardianship
							has_civic = civic_machine_tactical_algorithms
						}
					}
					force_add_civic = civic_machine_terminator
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_ethic = ethic_pacifist }
					shift_ethic = ethic_militarist
					country_event = { id = libra_origin.1106 days = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						or = {
							has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_pacifist
							has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian
						}
					}
					shift_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
				}
				change_government = {
					authority = random
					civics = random
					cooldown = no
					remove_invalid_civics = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_civil_education }
				force_remove_civic = civic_civil_education
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_corporate_hedonism }
				force_remove_civic = civic_corporate_hedonism
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_pleasure_seekers }
				force_remove_civic = civic_pleasure_seekers
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_beastmasters }
				force_remove_civic = civic_beastmasters
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_corporate_beastmasters }
				force_remove_civic = civic_corporate_beastmasters
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_catalytic_processing }
				force_remove_civic = civic_catalytic_processing
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_corporate_catalytic_processing }
				force_remove_civic = civic_corporate_catalytic_processing
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_public_relations_specialists }
				force_remove_civic = civic_public_relations_specialists
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_civic = civic_diplomatic_corps }
				force_remove_civic = civic_diplomatic_corps
				set_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = libra_civic_removed }
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_xenophobe
						or = {
							has_ethic = ethic_militarist
							has_ethic = ethic_spiritualist
						}
						nor = {
							has_civic = civic_barbaric_despoilers
							has_civic = civic_pompous_purists
							has_civic = civic_crusader_spirit
							has_civic = civic_selective_kinship
							has_civic = civic_sovereign_guardianship
							has_civic = civic_corporate_sovereign_guardianship
						}
					}
					force_add_civic = civic_fanatic_purifiers
				}
				set_government_cooldown = no
				add_resource = { unity = 750 }
			}
		}
		remove_country_flag = libra_civic_removed
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1110
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		owner = {
			subtract_variable = { which = libra_antientropy_instruction_index value = 60 }
			switch = {
				trigger = has_technology
				tech_libra_antientropy_index_2 = {
					root = {
						set_timed_planet_flag = {
							flag = recently_libra_antientropy_instruction_decision_ai
							days = 18
						}
					}
				}
				tech_libra_antientropy_index_1 = {
					root = {
						set_timed_planet_flag = {
							flag = recently_libra_antientropy_instruction_decision
							days = 30
						}
					}
				}
				default = {
					root = {
						set_timed_planet_flag = {
							flag = recently_libra_antientropy_instruction_decision
							days = 60
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		while = {
			count = 8
			random_list = {
				4 = {
					create_army = {
						owner = root.owner
						species = owner_main_species
						type = libra_antientropy_instruction_army_1
					}
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 2
						owner = { has_technology = tech_adaptive_combat_algorithms }
					}
					create_army = {
						owner = root.owner
						species = owner_main_species
						type = libra_antientropy_instruction_army_2
					}
				}
				0 = {
					modifier = {
						add = 1
						owner = { has_technology = tech_biomechanics }
					}
					create_army = {
						owner = root.owner
						species = owner_main_species
						type = libra_antientropy_instruction_army_3
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_planetarian
		has_civic = civic_machine_sovereign_guardianship
	}

	immediate = {
		give_technology = { tech = tech_lasers_2 message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_shields_2 message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_fusion_power message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_adaptive_combat_algorithms message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_mass_drivers_2 message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_torpedoes_1 message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_ship_armor_2 message = no }
		capital_scope = {
			set_timed_planet_flag = {
				flag = ignore_ai_building_limitations
				days = 1800
			}
			clear_deposits = yes
			add_deposit = d_junk_hollows
			add_deposit = d_junk_wastes
			add_deposit = d_ruined_building_blocker
			while = {
				count = 3
				add_deposit = d_junk_hollows
				add_deposit = d_junk_wastes
				add_deposit = d_junk_canals
				add_deposit = d_ruined_building_blocker
			}
			add_deposit = d_libra_planetarian_blocker
			add_deposit = d_libra_planetarian_blocker
			add_deposit = d_libra_planetarian
			add_building = building_libra_planetarian
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			kill_pop_group = {
				pop_group = this
				percentage = 0.35
			}
		}
		if = { 
			limit = {
				has_civic = civic_machine_servitor
			}
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					is_sapient = yes
					is_robot_pop_group = no
				}
				kill_pop_group = {
					pop_group = this
					amount = 800
				}
			}
			capital_scope = {
				remove_building = building_factory_1
				add_building = building_foundry_1
				create_pop_group = {
					species = root
					size = 800
				}
			}
		}
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.1201 days = 363 random = 21 }
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.1202 days = 18 random = 6 }
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.1220 days = 19440 }
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.1220 days = 36000 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1320
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_eden_accord
		has_valid_civic = civic_libra_eden_accord_f
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_modifier = libra_eden_accord_specimen_mod }
			export_modifier_duration_to_variable = {
				modifier = libra_eden_accord_specimen_mod
				variable = libra_eden_specimen
			}
			remove_modifier = libra_eden_accord_specimen_mod
		}
		change_variable = { which = libra_eden_specimen value = 1080 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = libra_eden_specimen value > 5400 }
			}
			set_variable = { which = libra_eden_specimen value = 5400 }
		}
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = libra_eden_accord_specimen_mod
			days = 1
			time_multiplier = libra_eden_specimen
		}
		clear_variable = libra_eden_specimen
	}
}

# miracle builder

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1500
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_miracle_builder
	}

	immediate = {
		add_tradition = tr_libra_cosmoplasty_adopt
		add_tradition = tr_libra_cosmoplasty_3
		capital_scope = {
			add_deposit = d_libra_miracle_builder
			add_building = building_libra_cosmoplasty_complex
		}
		give_technology = { tech = tech_dyson_swarm message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_starbase_3 message = no }
		give_technology = { tech = tech_starbase_4 message = no }
		if = {
			limit = { has_civic = civic_privatized_exploration }
			give_technology = { tech = tech_starbase_5 message = no }
		}
		else = {
			add_research_option = tech_starbase_5
		}
		add_research_option = tech_orbital_arc_furnace
		add_research_option = tech_orbital_ring_tier_1
		add_research_option = tech_habitat_1
		add_modifier = { modifier = libra_miracle_builder_effect_2 }
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_miracle_builder_count
			value = 0
		}
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = libra_miracle_builder_effect_1
			multiplier = 0
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1501
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_miracle_builder
	}

	immediate = {
		every_owned_planet = {
			limit = {
				is_under_colonization = no
				has_planetary_ascension_tier < 10
			}
			random = {
				chance = 20
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
			}
		}
		change_variable = {
			which = libra_miracle_builder_count
			value = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_miracle_builder_count
					value <= 50
				}
			}
			remove_modifier = libra_miracle_builder_effect_1
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = libra_miracle_builder_effect_1
				multiplier = libra_miracle_builder_count
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = libra_origin.1502
	hide_window = yes
	location = FROM
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		every_system_planet = {
			limit = {
				is_astral_scar = no
				colonizable_planet = no
				nor = {
					has_deposit = d_libra_miracle_builder_1
					has_deposit = d_libra_miracle_builder_2
					has_deposit = d_libra_starry_miner_1
					has_deposit = d_libra_starry_miner_2
					has_deposit = d_libra_starry_miner_3
					has_deposit = d_libra_starry_miner_4
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_deposit_for = shipclass_research_station }
				add_deposit = d_libra_miracle_builder_1
			}
			else = {
				add_deposit = d_libra_miracle_builder_2
			}
		}
	}
}

# extreme iteration

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1700
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_extreme_iteration
		species = { is_archetype = MACHINE }
		or = {
			has_ethic = ethic_materialist
			has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_materialist
			has_ethic = ethic_gestalt_consciousness
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		add_research_option = tech_colonization_2
		add_research_option = tech_robomodding_m
		capital_scope = {
			add_building = building_libra_extreme_iteration
			add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
			#add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_gestalt = no
				num_council_positions < 5
			}
			unlock_council_slots = 1
			set_council_position_to_council = councilor_libra_extreme_iteration
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_gestalt = yes }
			add_modifier = { modifier = libra_origin_gestalt_modifier }
		}
	}
}

# centurion

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1800
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_origin = origin_libra_centurion
		has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_militarist
	}

	immediate = {
		capital_scope = {
			add_building = building_libra_centurion_mirror
			add_building = building_foundry_1
		}
		country_event = { id = libra_origin.1804 days = 1620 }
		set_variable = { which = libra_centurion_count value = 1 }
		set_variable = { which = libra_centurion_decision value = 0 }
		every_owned_leader = { change_background_ethic = ethic_militarist }
		every_pool_leader = { change_background_ethic = ethic_militarist }
		unlock_council_selection = yes
		if = {
			limit = { num_council_positions < 5 }
			unlock_council_slots = 1
		}
		set_council_position_to_council = councilor_defense
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_councilor = no
				leader_class = commander
			}
			set_council_position = councilor_defense
		}
		set_council_position_to_council = councilor_research
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				is_councilor = no
				leader_class = scientist
			}
			set_council_position = councilor_research
		}
		set_council_position_to_council = councilor_libra_praetorian_commander
		random_owned_leader = {
			limit = { is_councilor = no }
			set_council_position = councilor_libra_praetorian_commander
			weights = {
				base = 1
				modifier = {
					add = 9
					leader_class = official
				}
			}
		}
		set_council_position_to_council = councilor_libra_primus_pilus
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_leader = {
					is_councilor = no
					leader_class = commander
				}
			}
			random_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					is_councilor = no
					leader_class = commander
				}
				set_council_position = councilor_libra_primus_pilus
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_pool_leader = {
				limit = { leader_class = commander }
				root = {
					clone_leader = {
						target = prev
						effect = { set_council_position = councilor_libra_primus_pilus }
					}
				}
				kill_leader = { show_notification = no }
			}
		}
		create_message = {
			type = LIBRA_CENTURION_ORIGIN_EFFECT
			localization = LIBRA_CENTURION_ORIGIN_EFFECT_DESC
			days = 60
			target = capital_scope
		}
	}
}

			value = 1
		}

		add_modifier = {
			modifier = decision_libra_centurion_mod
			mult = libra_centurion_decision
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

					which = libra_centurion_decision
					value > 24
				}

			add_resource = {
				influence = 100
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_resource = {
				influence = 4
				mult = libra_centurion_decision
			}
		}
	}
}

planet_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1811
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		while = {
			count = 3
			if = {
				limit = { has_planetary_ascension_tier < 9 }
				set_planetary_ascension_tier = value:add_planet_ascension_tier
			}
		}
		owner = {
			change_variable = {
				which = libra_centurion_decision
				value = 1
			}
			remove_modifier = decision_libra_centurion_mod
			add_modifier = {
				modifier = decision_libra_centurion_mod
				mult = libra_centurion_decision
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = libra_centurion_decision
						value > 24
					}
				}
				add_monthly_resource_mult = {
					resource = unity
					value = 6
					min = 3000
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_monthly_resource_mult = {
					resource = unity
					value = 0.25
					min = 120
					mult = libra_centurion_decision
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = libra_origin.1813
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = { has_base_skill < 10 }
			add_experience = root.value:libra_centurion_exp_reward
		}
		change_variable = {
			which = libra_centurion_decision
			value = 1
		}
		remove_modifier = decision_libra_centurion_mod
		add_modifier = {
			modifier = decision_libra_centurion_mod
			mult = libra_centurion_decision
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = libra_centurion_decision
					value > 24
				}
			}
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					has_base_skill < 10
					has_ethic = ethic_militarist
				}
				add_experience = 1000
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				which = libra_centurion_militarist_exp
				value  = libra_centurion_decision
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				which = libra_centurion_militarist_exp
				value  = 40
			}
			every_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					has_base_skill < 10
					has_ethic = ethic_militarist
				}
				add_experience = root.libra_centurion_militarist_exp
			}
			clear_variable = libra_centurion_militarist_exp
		}
	}
}
